Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the differential framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 716. The resulting operator achieves bound O(502/n^1).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_716(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 716. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^502(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a differential algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 559. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 465/5.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the differential decomposition. By minimality, the element a^559 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 465-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

Theorem 218.3 (Optimality Condition)

Statement: Under the optimality condition assumption on differential structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 236. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 516 with relations in degree 4.

For the inductive step, we decompose the differential structure using the optimality condition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 516. Each subproblem has a solution by the optimality condition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
